    def test_reopened_key_at_the_closure_block_gets_a_new_incident_id(tmp_path, plan):

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value", MALFORMED, ids=repr)
    def test_non_boolean_enabled_is_a_configuration_error_and_changes_nothing(tmp_path, plan, value):
        """D14: `checks.sw_a.enabled` = 0, null, "", "false" or 1 → `Worker()` raises, naming the check; the database
        is unchanged (no row closed, no marker, no outbox row, no run). On the base revision 0/null/"" close the four
        rows as `disabled` and "false"/1 build and run the check."""
        env = Env(tmp_path)
        open_four(env)
        db_path = env.w.cfg.db_path
        before = _dump(db_path)
        cfg = _switch_cfg(env, value)
        error = None
        try:
            built = registry.build(cfg.raw, only=["sw_a"])
            w = Worker(cfg, FakeRpc(env.chain), DB(cfg.db_path), env.notifier, clock=env.clock, checks=built)
            w.bootstrap()
            env.clock.advance(3600)
            env.chain.mine()
            advance(w)
        except SystemExit as e:
            error = e

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("command", ["run", "once", "verify-layout", "status"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value", MALFORMED, ids=repr)
    def test_non_boolean_enabled_stops_every_cli_command_that_builds_a_worker(tmp_path, plan, monkeypatch, command,
                                                                             value):
        """D14 through `main()`: the three start commands and the read-only `status` raise the configuration error
        before touching the database (`export` and `audit` build no `Worker` and read no switch)."""
        import eb_reconcile
    
        env = Env(tmp_path)
        open_four(env)
        db_path = env.w.cfg.db_path
        before = _dump(db_path)
        cfg = _switch_cfg(env, value)
        real_build, real_step = registry.build, Worker.step
        only = ["sw_a", "m5"] if command == "verify-layout" else ["sw_a"]
    
        def one_step(self, *args, **kwargs):
            real_step(self, *args, **kwargs)
            if command == "run":
                raise _Stopped
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(eb_reconcile, "load_config", lambda _: cfg)
        monkeypatch.setattr(eb_reconcile, "make_rpc", lambda _: FakeRpc(env.chain))
        monkeypatch.setattr(eb_reconcile, "make_notifier", lambda _: env.notifier)
        monkeypatch.setattr(eb_reconcile.signal, "signal", lambda *_: None)
        monkeypatch.setattr(registry, "build", lambda settings, only_=None: real_build(settings, only=only))
        monkeypatch.setattr(registry.REGISTRY["m5"], "verify_layout", lambda *a, **k: [])
        monkeypatch.setattr(Worker, "step", one_step)
        env.clock.advance(3600)
        env.chain.mine()
        error = None
        try:
            eb_reconcile.main([command, "--config", "unused"])
        except SystemExit as e:
            error = e
        except _Stopped:
            pass
        # behavioural assertions first (red on the base revision for the start commands: the rows are closed or the

        assert _states(db_path, "sw_a") == ["open"] * 4

___________ test_m1_reported_counts_a_message_of_a_disabled_incident ___________

tmp_path = PosixPath('/private/tmp/cs-cp2-qa3-cp1-red-dree8m68/test-tmp/test_m1_reported_counts_a_mess0')

    def test_m1_reported_counts_a_message_of_a_disabled_incident(tmp_path):
        """V1.6 reader `m1_stale_tuple.py` `_reported` (outbox JOIN incidents, any state), by design: a transient
        poisoning (poisoned and repaired between two runs) sent while M1 was enabled, its incident then closed as
        `disabled`, a reorg while M1 is off re-including the same two transactions: the re-enabled M1 re-detects it from
        the new chain and sends nothing, because the message naming the transaction was already sent (whatever the
        incident's later state). Matching only open/resolved incidents would send that warn a second time."""
        from fixtures_cluster import OPS, OWNER, deposit, m1_config, make_m1, poison
        from harness import VIEWS, make_worker
        from test_m1 import detections, incidents, kinds, restart
    
        chain, w, ssv = make_m1(tmp_path)
        deposit(ssv, value=10**18)
        advance(w)
        saved = dict(ssv._get(OWNER, OPS)[1])
        fork = chain.number
        tx, _, _ = poison(ssv)
        rep, _ = deposit(ssv)
        advance(w)
        assert kinds(w) == [("warn", "open")] and incidents(w, "open")  # reported once, still open
        _, off = make_worker(tmp_path, only=[], chain=chain, clock=w.clock, views=VIEWS, start_block=w.cfg.start_block,
                             activation_block=w.cfg.activation_block, checks={"m1": {**m1_config(), "enabled": False}})
        off.bootstrap()
        (old,) = incidents(off)
>       assert old["state"] == "disabled"
E       AssertionError: assert 'open' == 'disabled'
E         
E         - disabled
E         + open

reconcile/tests/test_switches.py:689: AssertionError
_________________________ test_seeded_switch_sweep[0] __________________________

tmp_path = PosixPath('/private/tmp/cs-cp2-qa3-cp1-red-dree8m68/test-tmp/test_seeded_switch_sweep_0_0')
plan = {'sw_a': []}, seed = 0

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(30))
    def test_seeded_switch_sweep(tmp_path, plan, seed):
        """Random restarts (enable/disable, backfill on/off), steps across the re-alert cadence, reorgs of depth 1-4,
        findings opening/escalating/resolving, an RPC outage at a start. Invariants after every action:
        - a disabled period creates no outbox row of the disabled check (no re-alert, no resolution, no notice);
        - while disabled: no `open` row of the check, the marker exists; while enabled: no marker;
        - a `disabled` row never changes again; at most one open row per key (the index)."""
        rnd = random.Random(seed)
        env = Env(tmp_path, confirmations=rnd.choice([0, 2]))
        keys = ["k1", "k2", "k3"]
        sev = ["info", "warn", "page"]
        PLAN["sw_a"] = []
        disabled = False
        env.start_worker(only=("sw_a", "sw_b"))
        frozen: dict[str, dict] = {}
        for _ in range(25):
            act = rnd.choice(["step", "step", "long", "finding", "restart", "reorg", "outage"])
            n_before = env.w.db.conn.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(rowid), 0) FROM outbox").fetchone()[0]
            if act == "step":
                env.step(rnd.choice([60, 600]))
            elif act == "long":
                env.step(7 * 3600)
            elif act == "finding":
                k = rnd.choice(keys)
                PLAN["sw_a"] = [f for f in PLAN["sw_a"] if f.key != k]
                if rnd.random() < 0.7:
                    PLAN["sw_a"].append(finding("sw_a", k, rnd.choice(sev), notify=rnd.random() < 0.8))
                else:
                    PLAN["sw_a"].append(finding("sw_a", k, "page", resolve=True))
                env.step()
            elif act == "restart" or act == "outage":
                disabled = rnd.random() < 0.5
                env.start_worker(only=("sw_a", "sw_b"), disabled=("sw_a",) if disabled else (),
                                 backfill=rnd.random() < 0.5, bootstrap=act == "restart")
                if act == "outage":
                    env.chain.down = True
                    env.clock.advance(7 * 3600)
                    env.w.step()
                    env.chain.down = False
                    env.w.step()
            elif act == "reorg" and env.chain.number - env.start > 6:
                env.chain.reorg(env.chain.number - rnd.randint(1, 4))
                env.step()
            if disabled:
                new = outbox(env.w, f"rowid > {n_before}")
                assert not [r for r in new if r["check_id"] == "sw_a"], (seed, act, new)
                assert not [r for r in env.rows("sw_a") if r["state"] == "open"], (seed, act)
>               assert env.w.db.meta("disabled:sw_a") is not None
E               AssertionError: assert None is not None
E                +  where None = meta('disabled:sw_a')
E                +    where meta = <db.DB object at 0x1078e12e0>.meta
E                +      where <db.DB object at 0x1078e12e0> = <eb_reconcile.Worker object at 0x1078e2300>.db
E                +        where <eb_reconcile.Worker object at 0x1078e2300> = <test_switches.Env object at 0x10798ebd0>.w

tmp_path = PosixPath('/private/tmp/cs-cp2-qa3-cp1-red-dree8m68/test-tmp/test_seeded_switch_sweep_5_0')
plan = {'sw_a': []}, seed = 5

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(30))
    def test_seeded_switch_sweep(tmp_path, plan, seed):
        """Random restarts (enable/disable, backfill on/off), steps across the re-alert cadence, reorgs of depth 1-4,
        findings opening/escalating/resolving, an RPC outage at a start. Invariants after every action:
        - a disabled period creates no outbox row of the disabled check (no re-alert, no resolution, no notice);
        - while disabled: no `open` row of the check, the marker exists; while enabled: no marker;
        - a `disabled` row never changes again; at most one open row per key (the index)."""
        rnd = random.Random(seed)
        env = Env(tmp_path, confirmations=rnd.choice([0, 2]))
        keys = ["k1", "k2", "k3"]
        sev = ["info", "warn", "page"]
        PLAN["sw_a"] = []
        disabled = False
        env.start_worker(only=("sw_a", "sw_b"))
        frozen: dict[str, dict] = {}
        for _ in range(25):
            act = rnd.choice(["step", "step", "long", "finding", "restart", "reorg", "outage"])
            n_before = env.w.db.conn.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(rowid), 0) FROM outbox").fetchone()[0]
            if act == "step":
                env.step(rnd.choice([60, 600]))
            elif act == "long":
                env.step(7 * 3600)
            elif act == "finding":
                k = rnd.choice(keys)
                PLAN["sw_a"] = [f for f in PLAN["sw_a"] if f.key != k]
                if rnd.random() < 0.7:
                    PLAN["sw_a"].append(finding("sw_a", k, rnd.choice(sev), notify=rnd.random() < 0.8))
                else:
                    PLAN["sw_a"].append(finding("sw_a", k, "page", resolve=True))
                env.step()
            elif act == "restart" or act == "outage":
                disabled = rnd.random() < 0.5
                env.start_worker(only=("sw_a", "sw_b"), disabled=("sw_a",) if disabled else (),
                                 backfill=rnd.random() < 0.5, bootstrap=act == "restart")
                if act == "outage":
                    env.chain.down = True
                    env.clock.advance(7 * 3600)
                    env.w.step()
                    env.chain.down = False
                    env.w.step()
            elif act == "reorg" and env.chain.number - env.start > 6:
                env.chain.reorg(env.chain.number - rnd.randint(1, 4))
                env.step()
            if disabled:
                new = outbox(env.w, f"rowid > {n_before}")
                assert not [r for r in new if r["check_id"] == "sw_a"], (seed, act, new)
                assert not [r for r in env.rows("sw_a") if r["state"] == "open"], (seed, act)
>               assert env.w.db.meta("disabled:sw_a") is not None
E               AssertionError: assert None is not None
E                +  where None = meta('disabled:sw_a')
E                +    where meta = <db.DB object at 0x107825610>.meta
E                +      where <db.DB object at 0x107825610> = <eb_reconcile.Worker object at 0x107827650>.db
E                +        where <eb_reconcile.Worker object at 0x107827650> = <test_switches.Env object at 0x1078240b0>.w

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(30))
    def test_seeded_switch_sweep(tmp_path, plan, seed):
        """Random restarts (enable/disable, backfill on/off), steps across the re-alert cadence, reorgs of depth 1-4,
        findings opening/escalating/resolving, an RPC outage at a start. Invariants after every action:
        - a disabled period creates no outbox row of the disabled check (no re-alert, no resolution, no notice);
        - while disabled: no `open` row of the check, the marker exists; while enabled: no marker;
        - a `disabled` row never changes again; at most one open row per key (the index)."""
        rnd = random.Random(seed)
        env = Env(tmp_path, confirmations=rnd.choice([0, 2]))
        keys = ["k1", "k2", "k3"]
        sev = ["info", "warn", "page"]
        PLAN["sw_a"] = []
        disabled = False
        env.start_worker(only=("sw_a", "sw_b"))
        frozen: dict[str, dict] = {}
        for _ in range(25):
            act = rnd.choice(["step", "step", "long", "finding", "restart", "reorg", "outage"])
            n_before = env.w.db.conn.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(rowid), 0) FROM outbox").fetchone()[0]
            if act == "step":
                env.step(rnd.choice([60, 600]))
            elif act == "long":
                env.step(7 * 3600)
            elif act == "finding":
                k = rnd.choice(keys)
                PLAN["sw_a"] = [f for f in PLAN["sw_a"] if f.key != k]
                if rnd.random() < 0.7:
                    PLAN["sw_a"].append(finding("sw_a", k, rnd.choice(sev), notify=rnd.random() < 0.8))
                else:
                    PLAN["sw_a"].append(finding("sw_a", k, "page", resolve=True))
                env.step()

                disabled = rnd.random() < 0.5
                env.start_worker(only=("sw_a", "sw_b"), disabled=("sw_a",) if disabled else (),
                                 backfill=rnd.random() < 0.5, bootstrap=act == "restart")
                if act == "outage":
                    env.chain.down = True
                    env.clock.advance(7 * 3600)
                    env.w.step()
                    env.chain.down = False
                    env.w.step()
            elif act == "reorg" and env.chain.number - env.start > 6:
                env.chain.reorg(env.chain.number - rnd.randint(1, 4))
                env.step()

_________________________ test_seeded_switch_sweep[19] _________________________

tmp_path = PosixPath('/private/tmp/cs-cp2-qa3-cp1-red-dree8m68/test-tmp/test_seeded_switch_sweep_19_0')
plan = {'sw_a': []}, seed = 19

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(30))
    def test_seeded_switch_sweep(tmp_path, plan, seed):
        """Random restarts (enable/disable, backfill on/off), steps across the re-alert cadence, reorgs of depth 1-4,
        findings opening/escalating/resolving, an RPC outage at a start. Invariants after every action:
        - a disabled period creates no outbox row of the disabled check (no re-alert, no resolution, no notice);
        - while disabled: no `open` row of the check, the marker exists; while enabled: no marker;
        - a `disabled` row never changes again; at most one open row per key (the index)."""
        rnd = random.Random(seed)
        env = Env(tmp_path, confirmations=rnd.choice([0, 2]))
        keys = ["k1", "k2", "k3"]
        sev = ["info", "warn", "page"]
        PLAN["sw_a"] = []
        disabled = False
        env.start_worker(only=("sw_a", "sw_b"))
        frozen: dict[str, dict] = {}
        for _ in range(25):
            act = rnd.choice(["step", "step", "long", "finding", "restart", "reorg", "outage"])
            n_before = env.w.db.conn.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(rowid), 0) FROM outbox").fetchone()[0]
            if act == "step":
                env.step(rnd.choice([60, 600]))
            elif act == "long":
                env.step(7 * 3600)
            elif act == "finding":
                k = rnd.choice(keys)
                PLAN["sw_a"] = [f for f in PLAN["sw_a"] if f.key != k]
                if rnd.random() < 0.7:
                    PLAN["sw_a"].append(finding("sw_a", k, rnd.choice(sev), notify=rnd.random() < 0.8))
                else:
                    PLAN["sw_a"].append(finding("sw_a", k, "page", resolve=True))
                env.step()
            elif act == "restart" or act == "outage":
                disabled = rnd.random() < 0.5
                env.start_worker(only=("sw_a", "sw_b"), disabled=("sw_a",) if disabled else (),
                                 backfill=rnd.random() < 0.5, bootstrap=act == "restart")
                if act == "outage":
                    env.chain.down = True
                    env.clock.advance(7 * 3600)
                    env.w.step()
                    env.chain.down = False
                    env.w.step()
            elif act == "reorg" and env.chain.number - env.start > 6:
                env.chain.reorg(env.chain.number - rnd.randint(1, 4))
                env.step()
            if disabled:
                new = outbox(env.w, f"rowid > {n_before}")
                assert not [r for r in new if r["check_id"] == "sw_a"], (seed, act, new)
                assert not [r for r in env.rows("sw_a") if r["state"] == "open"], (seed, act)
>               assert env.w.db.meta("disabled:sw_a") is not None
E               AssertionError: assert None is not None
E                +  where None = meta('disabled:sw_a')
E                +    where meta = <db.DB object at 0x1078e2810>.meta
E                +      where <db.DB object at 0x1078e2810> = <eb_reconcile.Worker object at 0x107aa34a0>.db
E                +        where <eb_reconcile.Worker object at 0x107aa34a0> = <test_switches.Env object at 0x1079139b0>.w
